## Interpretation receipt

Opcode 2 is the public/address name lane: every one of its 4,503 rows
appears at the exact same address in the independent retail MAP.  Its
complete typed subset pairs with EXT, contrasting with opcode 6's STAT
subset.  Untyped rows retain public vendor/ABI/data names without enough
debug information to infer original C declarations.
